We verify our Latent Color Subspace (LCS) interpretation by demonstrating that it can both predict and explicitly control color, introducing a fully training-free method in FLUX based solely on closed-form latent-space manipulation. Code is available at https://github.com/ExplainableML/LCS.

</details>


### [398] [Sample-Efficient Hypergradient Estimation for Decentralized Bi-Level Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.14867)
*Mikoto Kudo,Takumi Tanabe,Akifumi Wachi,Youhei Akimoto*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对双层强化学习中的去中心化场景（领导者无法干预跟随者优化过程，只能观察优化结果），利用玻尔兹曼协方差技巧推导了超梯度的新形式，使得仅从交互样本即可高效估计超梯度，无需重复状态访问或高维梯度估计器，并首次将超梯度优化扩展到去中心化的双人马尔可夫博弈中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双层强化学习中，领导者无法干预跟随者的优化过程，只能观察其优化结果，现有超梯度方法要么需要大量重复状态访问数据，要么梯度估计器复杂度随领导者决策空间维数急剧增长，缺乏高效的去中心化求解方法。

Method: 利用玻尔兹曼协方差技巧推导超梯度的替代形式，仅从交互样本中估计超梯度，避免对重复状态访问或高维梯度估计器的依赖。

Result: 所提方法在离散和连续状态任务中均有效，超梯度更新能显著提升优化效果。

Conclusion: 该方法是首个在去中心化设置下实现双人马尔可夫博弈超梯度优化的方法，为仓库机器人环境设计等战略性决策问题提供了高效求解途径。

Abstract: Many strategic decision-making problems, such as environment design for warehouse robots, can be naturally formulated as bi-level reinforcement learning (RL), where a leader agent optimizes its objective while a follower solves a Markov decision process (MDP) conditioned on the leader's decisions. In many situations, a fundamental challenge arises when the leader cannot intervene in the follower's optimization process; it can only observe the optimization outcome. We address this decentralized setting by deriving the hypergradient of the leader's objective, i.e., the gradient of the leader's strategy that accounts for changes in the follower's optimal policy. Unlike prior hypergradient-based methods that require extensive data for repeated state visits or rely on gradient estimators whose complexity can increase substantially with the high-dimensional leader's decision space, we leverage the Boltzmann covariance trick to derive an alternative hypergradient formulation. This enables efficient hypergradient estimation solely from interaction samples, even when the leader's decision space is high-dimensional. Additionally, to our knowledge, this is the first method that enables hypergradient-based optimization for 2-player Markov games in decentralized settings. Experiments highlight the impact of hypergradient updates and demonstrate our method's effectiveness in both discrete and continuous state tasks.

</details>


### [399] [Point-Identification of a Robust Predictor Under Latent Shift with Imperfect Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.15158)
*Zahra Rahiminasab,Reza Soumi,Arto Klami,Samuel Kaski*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出潜在等价类（LECs）概念，用于处理存在潜在混淆变量时的域适应问题。通过在跨域混合权重上施加秩条件，即使在代理变量不完美（不可点识别）的情况下，也能实现鲁棒预测器的点识别。进一步提出主动查询框架PQAL，选择满足该秩条件的多样化域，在合成和半合成数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于代理变量的方法依赖“完备性假设”（completeness）来唯一确定（点识别）鲁棒预测器。当代理变量不完美时，该假设不成立，导致多解（集识别）问题。本文旨在放宽这一强假设，在更弱的条件下实现点识别。

Method: 1) 引入潜在等价类（LECs），将映射到相同条件代理分布的潜在混淆变量归为一组；2) 证明当多个域在混合LECs形成鲁棒预测器的权重上存在足够差异（跨域秩条件）时，点识别仍可实现；3) 提出Proximal Quasi-Bayesian Active Learning (PQAL)框架，主动查询少量满足该秩条件的多样化域。

Result: 在合成数据及半合成数据集（dSprites、IHDP、ACS Folktables）上，PQAL恢复了点识别预测器，对不同迁移程度具有鲁棒性，且性能优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 跨域混合权重的多样性（秩条件）可替代传统完备性假设，实现不完美代理变量下的点识别；主动查询策略能高效获取满足条件的域。

Abstract: Addressing the domain adaptation problem becomes more challenging when distribution shifts across domains stem from latent confounders that affect both covariates and outcomes. Existing proxy-based approaches that address latent shift rely on a strong completeness assumption to uniquely determine (point-identify) a robust predictor. Completeness requires that proxies have sufficient information about variations in latent confounders. For imperfect proxies the mapping from confounders to the space of proxy distributions is non-injective, and multiple latent confounder values can generate the same proxy distribution. This breaks the completeness assumption and observed data are consistent with multiple potential predictors (set-identified). To address this, we introduce latent equivalent classes (LECs). LECs are defined as groups of latent confounders that induce the same conditional proxy distribution. We show that point-identification for the robust predictor remains achievable as long as multiple domains differ sufficiently in how they mix proxy-induced LECs to form the robust predictor. This domain diversity condition is formalized as a cross-domain rank condition on the mixture weights, which is substantially weaker assumption than completeness.
  We introduce the Proximal Quasi-Bayesian Active learning (PQAL) framework, which actively queries
  a small, targeted set of diverse domains that satisfy this rank condition. PQAL can recover the point-identified predictor, demonstrates robustness to varying degrees of shift and outperforms previous methods on synthetic data and semi-synthetic dSprites, IHDP, ACS Folktables datasets.

</details>


### [400] [Mechanisms of Introspective Awareness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2603.21396)
*Uzay Macar,Li Yang,Atticus Wang,Peter Wallich,Emmanuel Ameisen,Jack Lindsey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM在残差流中被注入操控向量时的"内省感知"能力。发现该能力主要源于后训练阶段的偏好优化（如DPO），而非标准微调；机制上存在一个两阶段电路：早期层"证据载体"特征检测扰动，抑制下游"门控"特征实现默认否定回答。该电路在基座模型中缺失，且对拒绝消融具有鲁棒性。通过消融拒绝方向或训练偏置向量可大幅提升检测性能（+53%和+75%），表明该能力被显著低估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究LLM在残差流中被注入操控向量时，能否检测到这种注入并识别注入概念（即"内省感知"现象），以及理解其背后的机制。

Method: 通过行为实验验证鲁棒性；比较不同训练方式（DPO vs SFT）对能力的影响；运用电路分析追踪检测机制的两阶段电路（证据载体特征→门控特征）；通过消融实验和偏置向量训练验证能力的可放大性。

Result: 模型能以中等速率检测注入向量且零假阳性；该能力源于DPO等偏好优化而非SFT；检测机制涉及早期层证据载体特征的单调扰动检测和下游门控特征的抑制；该电路在基座模型中缺失且对拒绝消融鲁棒；拒绝消融提升检测53%，训练偏置向量提升75%。

Conclusion: LLM的内省感知能力是鲁棒且机制上非平凡的，且当前被显著低估，未来模型可通过适当方法大幅增强该能力。

Abstract: Recent work has shown that LLMs can sometimes detect when steering vectors are injected into their residual stream and identify the injected concept -- a phenomenon termed "introspective awareness." We investigate the mechanisms underlying this capability in open-weights models. First, we find that it is behaviorally robust: models detect injected steering vectors at moderate rates with 0% false positives across diverse prompts and dialogue formats. Notably, this capability emerges specifically from post-training; we show that preference optimization algorithms like DPO can elicit it, but standard supervised finetuning does not. We provide evidence that detection cannot be explained by simple linear association between certain steering vectors and directions promoting affirmative responses. We trace the detection mechanism to a two-stage circuit in which "evidence carrier" features in early post-injection layers detect perturbations monotonically along diverse directions, suppressing downstream "gate" features that implement a default negative response. This circuit is absent in base models and robust to refusal ablation. Identification of injected concepts relies on largely distinct later-layer mechanisms that only weakly overlap with those involved in detection. Finally, we show that introspective capability is substantially underelicited: ablating refusal directions improves detection by +53%, and a trained bias vector improves it by +75% on held-out concepts, both without meaningfully increasing false positives. Our results suggest that this introspective awareness of injected concepts is robust and mechanistically nontrivial, and could be substantially amplified in future models. Code: https://github.com/safety-research/introspection-mechanisms.

</details>


### [401] [Estimating Tail Risks in Language Model Output Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.22167)
*Rico Angell,Raghav Singhal,Zachary Horvitz,Zhou Yu,Rajesh Ranganath,Kathleen McKeown,He He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于重要性采样的方法，通过构建目标模型的不安全版本来高效估计语言模型输出有害内容的尾部风险概率（如10^-4量级），仅需500个样本即可达到传统暴力采样10-20倍样本量的估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的安全评估方法仅关注产生有害输出的输入分布，忽视了语言模型的概率本质及其尾部输出行为。当模型每天被查询数十亿次时，即使是罕见的有害行为也会发生，因此需要有效的尾部风险估计方法。

Method: 通过构建目标模型的不安全版本来实现重要性采样，使有害输出更易出现，从而进行样本高效的罕见事件概率估计。

Result: 在误用和失调基准测试上，该方法使用传统暴力蒙特卡洛估计10-20倍的样本量即可达到同等精度；仅需500个样本即可估计概率量级为10^-4的有害输出。

Conclusion: 准确估计罕见事件概率对安全评估既关键又可行，该方法还能揭示模型对输入扰动的敏感性并预测部署风险。

Abstract: Language models are increasingly capable and are being rapidly deployed on a population-level scale. As a result, the safety of these models is increasingly high-stakes. Fortunately, advances in alignment have significantly reduced the likelihood of harmful model outputs. However, when models are queried billions of times in a day, even rare worst-case behaviors will occur. Current safety evaluations focus on capturing the distribution of inputs that yield harmful outputs. These evaluations disregard the probabilistic nature of models and their tail output behavior. To measure this tail risk, we propose a method to efficiently estimate the probability of harmful outputs for any input query. Instead of naive brute-force sampling from the target model, where harmful outputs could be rare, we operationalize importance sampling by creating unsafe versions of the target model. These unsafe versions enable sample-efficient estimation by making harmful outputs more probable. On benchmarks measuring misuse and misalignment, these estimates match brute-force Monte Carlo estimates using 10-20x fewer samples. For example, we can estimate probability of harmful outputs on the order of 10^-4 with just 500 samples. Additionally, we find that these harmfulness estimates can reveal the sensitivity of models to perturbations in model input and predict deployment risks. Our work demonstrates that accurate rare-event estimation is both critical and feasible for safety evaluations. Code is available at https://github.com/rangell/LMTailRisk

</details>


### [402] [Beyond Continuity: Simulation-free Reconstruction of Discrete Branching Dynamics from Single-cell Snapshots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.00545)
*Junda Ying,Yuxuan Wang,Bowen Yang,Peijie Zhou,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: USB提出了一种无模拟的框架，用于学习包含随机性和非平衡（细胞增殖与凋亡）效应的细胞轨迹，通过建模离散的出生-死亡跳跃动力学，在单细胞分辨率下实现了分支Schrödinger桥问题的可解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有非平衡最优传输方法将质量视为连续流体，在群体层面进行推断，无法捕捉单细胞分辨率下出生-死亡事件的离散跳跃特性，而这对于理解谱系分支和命运决定至关重要。

Method: 提出无模拟训练框架，引入模拟无关的训练目标，有效扩展到高维组学数据；理论上提供了分支Schrödinger桥问题的可解方案，将单细胞行为建模为布朗运动与离散出生-死亡跳跃的结合。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据集上，USB的轨迹重建性能达到或优于确定性基线方法，并且能够独特地实现单细胞分辨率的离散出生-死亡动力学真实模拟。

Conclusion: USB为单细胞轨迹推断提供了一种同时处理随机性和非平衡质量动力学的有效框架，兼具理论严谨性和实际可扩展性，在单细胞分辨率下实现了离散出生-死亡动力学的可解释建模。

Abstract: Inferring cellular trajectories from destructive snapshots is complicated by the challenges of stochasticity and non-conservative mass dynamics such as cell proliferation and apoptosis. Existing unbalanced Optimal Transport (OT) methods treat mass as a continuous fluid, performing inference at the population level. However, this macroscopic view often fails to capture the discrete, jump-like nature of birth-death events at single-cell resolution, which is essential for understanding lineage branching and fate decisions. We present Unbalanced Schrödinger Bridge (USB), a simulation-free framework for learning underlying dynamics that effectively integrates both stochastic and unbalanced effects which also models the discrete, jump-like birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution. Theoretically, USB provides a tractable solution to the Branching Schrödinger Bridge (BSB) problem, offering a rigorous microscopic interpretation where individual cells undergo both Brownian motion and discrete birth-death jumps. Technically, the method implements an efficient solver by introducing a simulation-free training objective that effectively scales to high-dimensional omics data. Empirically, we demonstrate on both simulated and real-world datasets that USB not only achieves trajectory reconstruction performance better than or comparable to deterministic baselines but also uniquely enables realistic discrete simulation of birth-death dynamics at single-cell resolution.

</details>


### [403] [Hybrid Iterative Neural Low-Regularity Integrator for Nonlinear Dispersive Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04853)
*Zhangyong Liang,Huanhuan Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HIN-LRI是一个混合框架，将经典数值求解器与神经算子相结合，通过校正结构截断误差来提升非线性色散PDE的求解精度，在理论保证和实验效果上均优于纯解析或纯神经方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统数值求解器在处理非线性色散PDE时存在结构截断误差，而纯神经网络替代方法缺乏理论保证和泛化能力。本文旨在通过混合方法结合二者的优势，利用神经算子学习解析方法无法闭合的残差缺陷，同时保持严格的收敛性理论保证。

Method: 提出HIN-LRI框架：1) 基础层使用低正则性积分器提供一致的一阶近似；2) 轻量级神经网络在低维潜在流形上学习残差校正；3) 显式时间步长缩放确保神经校正的Lipschitz贡献为𝒪(τ)，实现Gronwall稳定性因子关于步长和空间分辨率的均匀有界性；4) 通过solver-in-the-loop端到端训练，在Bourgain型范数中惩罚轨迹误差，对齐多步求解器动力学。

Result: 在粗糙数据的三个色散基准上，HIN-LRI在精度上优于解析积分器、分裂方法和神经PDE代理模型；展现出稳定的空间细化能力、有效的分布外迁移性能和适度的在线计算开销。全局误差满足C(ε_net+δ) τ^γ ln(1/τ)的理论界。

Conclusion: HIN-LRI成功弥合了数值分析与深度学习之间的鸿沟，为非线性PDE求解提供了一种兼具理论严谨性与数据驱动灵活性的混合范式，证明了通过精心设计的神经校正可以在保持严格收敛保证的同时显著提升经典求解器的性能。

Abstract: We propose HIN-LRI, a hybrid framework that augments a classical numerical solver with a neural operator trained to correct the solver's structured truncation error. A base low-regularity integrator provides a consistent first-order approximation to nonlinear dispersive PDEs, while a lightweight neural network, operating on a low-dimensional latent manifold, learns the residual defect that analytical methods cannot close. An explicit time-step scaling on the neural correction ensures that its Lipschitz contribution remains $\mathcal{O}(τ)$, yielding a Gronwall stability factor bounded uniformly in the step size and independent of the spatial resolution. The network is trained end-to-end through a solver-in-the-loop objective that unrolls the full iteration and penalises trajectory error in a Bourgain-type norm, aligning learning with multi-step solver dynamics rather than isolated one-step targets. Under stated assumptions, the global error satisfies $C(\varepsilon_{net}+δ)\,τ^γ\ln(1/τ)$, where $\varepsilon_{net}$ measures the network approximation quality and $δ$ the training shortfall. Experiments on three dispersive benchmarks with rough data show that HIN-LRI improves accuracy over analytical integrators, splitting methods, and neural PDE surrogates, with stable spatial refinement, effective out-of-distribution transfer, and modest online overhead.

</details>


### [404] [Self-Attention as Transport: Limits of Symmetric Spectral Diagnostics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.04893)
*Dominik Dahlem,Diego Maniloff,Mac Misiura*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于注意力路由失效形状（过度集中 vs. 过度扩散）的幻觉诊断框架，证明了谱方法的"方向盲"定理（无法区分算子与其转置），推导了因果注意力的Cheeger下界（φ≥1/5），并构建了容量-方向（φ-G）双轴诊断体系，在多个模型上取得0.62-0.84的LC-AUROC，且极性反转预测得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言模型在处理幻觉响应时，注意力路由会呈现出两种失效模式：过度集中在少数位置或过度扩散导致相关性稀释。作者希望利用这种失效形状的差异作为诊断信号，并解决现有谱方法无法捕捉信息流方向的根本性局限。

Method: 1) 在强制评分模式下从注意力矩阵提取诊断特征；2) 证明谱方法的转置不变性导致方向盲（orientation-blind），并用不对称系数G量化；3) 推导因果注意力的二分Cheeger常数闭式解，证明均匀因果注意力下界φ≥1/5，窗口注意力则突破下界至O(w/n)；4) 构建φ（容量）和G（方向）双轴诊断，预测瓶颈型与扩散型基准数据集极性相反。

Result: 在长度控制评估下，传输特征在不同架构（仅解码器、仅编码器、编码器-解码器）上保持0.62-0.84的LC-AUROC；HaluEval与MedHallu之间的极性反转验证了理论预测。

Conclusion: 注意力路由的失效形状（而非单纯数值差异）包含可诊断信号；谱诊断的方向盲性可通过不对称系数G弥补；Cheeger下界可作为理想化架构基准；双轴诊断具有跨架构泛化能力和可检验的极性预测。

Abstract: When a language model processes a hallucinated response, its attention routing tends to fail in one of two shapes: over-concentrating on a narrow set of positions, or spreading so diffusely that relevance is diluted, and the shape of the failure carries diagnostic signal. We study these shapes as a diagnostic characterization, computed from attention matrices under \emph{forced scoring} of benchmark-labeled responses rather than during live generation. A widely used family of spectral methods analyzes the symmetric component of the degree-normalized attention operator, which governs transport \emph{capacity}; we prove that every transpose-invariant spectral diagnostic of this operator is structurally \emph{orientation-blind} (it cannot distinguish an operator from its transpose, and therefore cannot detect information-flow direction), with a converse to the blindness theorem bounding any Lipschitz diagnostic's transpose sensitivity by the asymmetry coefficient $G$.
  Pairing this with a closed-form bipartite-Cheeger landscape for canonical causal architectures, we show that uniform causal attention satisfies an $n$-independent floor $φ\ge 1/5$, while window attention pierces the floor as $O(w/n)$; failure modes are shape-different, not just value-different. This floor is an idealized-architecture benchmark, not an empirical attractor: the fraction of real attention heads that pierce it is itself an architectural signature. The resulting two-axis diagnostic ($φ$ for capacity, $G$ for direction) yields a falsifiable polarity prediction: bottleneck- and diffuse-dominated benchmarks should exhibit opposite polarity. Under length-controlled evaluation, transport features retain interpretable signal (0.62-0.84 LC-AUROC) across the tested decoder-only, encoder-only, and encoder-decoder models, with polarity reversing as predicted between HaluEval and MedHallu.

</details>


### [405] [Understanding Sample Efficiency in Predictive Coding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11911)
*Gaspard Oliviers,Elene Lominadze,Rafal Bogacz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文通过"目标对齐"指标量化了预测编码（PC）与反向传播（BP）在学习效率上的差异，推导并验证了深度线性网络中目标对齐的解析表达式，发现PC比BP学习更高效，尤其在深、窄和预训练网络中更为明显，并推导了保证PC最优目标对齐的精确条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已有实验表明预测编码（PC）相比反向传播（BP）具有样本效率更高、学习效果更好等优势，但缺乏对其背后机制的理论理解，作者希望通过量化指标来解释这一现象。

Method: 提出"目标对齐"（target alignment）指标来衡量网络输出变化与输出预测误差的对齐程度；在深度线性网络中推导并实验验证了目标对齐的解析表达式；推导了保证PC最优目标对齐的精确条件；在线性和非线性模型的全训练轨迹上进行实验验证。

Result: PC的学习效率高于BP，且在深、窄和预训练网络中优势更明显；推导并验证了目标对齐的解析表达式及最优对齐条件；即使在部分假设被违反的情况下，PC的预测优势在实践中仍然存在。

Conclusion: 本研究为PC相比BP具有更高学习效率提供了机制层面的理论解释，并可为PC的参数化设置以最大化学习效果提供指导。

Abstract: Predictive Coding (PC) is an influential account of cortical learning. Much of recent work has focused on comparing PC to Backpropagation (BP) to find whether PC offers any advantages. Small scale experiments show that PC enables learning that is more sample efficient and effective in many contexts, though a thorough theoretical understanding of the phenomena remains elusive. To address this, we quantify the efficiency of learning in BP and PC through a metric called ``target alignment'', which measures how closely the change in the output of the network is aligned to the output prediction error. We then derive and empirically validate analytical expressions for target alignment in Deep Linear Networks. We show that learning in PC is more efficient than BP, which is especially pronounced in deep, narrow and pre-trained networks. We also derive exact conditions for guaranteed optimal target alignment in PC and validate our findings through experiments. We study full training trajectories of linear and non-linear models, and find the predicted benefits of PC persist in practice even when some assumptions are violated. Overall, this work provides a mechanistic understanding of the higher learning efficiency observed for PC over BP in previous works, and can guide how PC should be parametrised to learn most effectively.

</details>


### [406] [TAPIOCA: Why Task- Aware Pruning Improves OOD model Capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14738)
*Krish Sharma,Omar Naim,Soumadeep Saha,Vinija Jain,Aman Chadha,Nicholas Asher*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探讨任务感知层剪枝的作用机制，发现该方法对分布内数据无益，但能持续提升分布外数据的准确性。通过几何解释：OOD输入会扭曲任务适配的几何结构，剪枝移除产生或放大这种扭曲的层，使OOD表示向适配分布对齐，从而提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（如TALE）表明任务感知层剪枝能提升特定任务的模型性能，但其何时有效以及为何有效尚不明确。本文旨在探究这种改进发生的条件和背后的原因。

Method: 在受控多项式回归任务和大语言模型上进行实验，对比ID和OOD数据下的剪枝效果；分析层规范化和成对距离剖面在ID和OOD输入下的差异；通过受控分布偏移和残差缩放干预提供因果证据。

Result: 任务感知层剪枝对ID数据无改进，但持续提升OOD准确率；OOD输入会导致层规范化与成对距离剖面偏离ID剖面；剪枝通过移除扭曲任务适配几何的层，使OOD表示向适配分布对齐。

Conclusion: 任务感知层剪枝的本质是通过移除那些产生或放大OOD几何扭曲的层，将OOD输入的表示重新对齐到模型在任务上适配的几何结构，从而提升OOD性能。

Abstract: Recent work has promoted task-aware layer pruning as a way to improve model performance on particular tasks, as shown by TALE. In this paper, we investigate when such improvements occur and why. We show first that, across controlled polynomial regression tasks and large language models, such pruning yields no benefit on in-distribution (ID) data but consistently improves out-of-distribution (OOD) accuracy. We further show empirically that OOD inputs induce layerwise norm and pairwise-distance profiles that deviate from the corresponding ID profiles. This leads to a geometric explanation of task-aware pruning: each task induces a task-adapted geometry, characterized empirically by the representation profiles observed on ID inputs. OOD inputs can introduce a distorted version of the task-adapted geometry. Task-aware pruning identifies layers that create or amplify this distortion; by removing them, it shifts OOD representational norms and pairwise distances toward those observed on the adapted distribution. This realigns OOD inputs with the model's task-adapted geometry and improves performance. We provide causal evidence through controlled distribution shifts and residual-scaling interventions, and demonstrate consistent behavior across model scales.

</details>


### [407] [On the Stability of Growth in Structural Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15435)
*Lute Lillo,Nick Cheney*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度学习中网络结构增长（Grow）与剪枝（Prune）的非对称性，发现新增单元存在“前向激活但反向梯度饥饿”问题，在复杂视觉任务中增长的表现受限于新单元与已有优化轨迹的整合稳定性，而剪枝在最终性能上更具优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准深度学习通常在训练前固定网络架构，但自适应和持续学习系统需要动态调整结构（如剪枝或增长）。虽然增长在理论上具有吸引力，但其与剪枝并非简单互逆过程——剪枝操作的对象是已参与完整训练的单元，而增长则向已高度特化的优化轨迹中插入新单元，这种不对称性导致的新生单元整合问题尚未被系统研究。

Method: 通过对比实验系统隔离分析了新增单元的“插入问题”，设计了多个干预实验（优化器状态、插入策略、选择机制、可训练性等），在小型MLP基准和复杂图像分类任务（含卷积骨干）上分别评估Grow和Prune的表现，同时引入持续学习基准测试考察塑性损失场景。

Result: 1）新增单元在前向传播中处于激活状态，但反向传播梯度信号显著弱于已有单元（前向激活-反向饥饿）；2）该问题在小型MLP中影响较小，但在含卷积骨干的复杂图像分类中差异显著；3）Grow在结构编辑过程中能达到高最终准确率，但若按训练轨迹平均或对最终稀疏网络从头训练，Prune表现更强；4）改善新生单元整合能提升自适应性能，但不会自动产生更好的最终子网络；5）在持续学习中，Grow仅在新单元有足够时间整合时才具备竞争力。

Conclusion: 网络增长不应仅被视为架构搜索算子，而是一个依赖插入稳定性的时间敏感优化过程。增长的成功关键不在于简单增加参数，而在于如何确保新单元能够有效融入已有的优化轨迹。

Abstract: Standard deep-learning pipelines usually choose the network architecture before training and keep it fixed throughout optimization. In contrast, a model can also be adapted by editing its structure during training, for example by pruning existing hidden-neuron units or growing new ones. Although growth is appealing for adaptive and continual systems, we show that it is not simply the inverse of pruning. Pruning selects among units that have participated in training from the start, whereas growth inserts new units into an already specialized optimization trajectory. We isolate this insertion problem and show that newborn units are often forward-active but backward-starved: they participate in the forward computation, yet receive much weaker gradient signal than incumbent units. This disadvantage is minor in small MLP benchmarks, but becomes clear in harder image-classification settings with a convolutional trunk. In these settings, \textsc{Grow} can achieve high final accuracy during the structural-editing procedure, while \textsc{Prune} is stronger when performance is averaged over the training trajectory or when the final sparse network is retrained from scratch. Interventions targeting optimizer state, insertion, selection, and trainability show that improving the integration of newborn units can improve adaptive performance, but does not automatically produce better final subnetworks. In continual-learning benchmarks stressing plasticity loss, \textsc{Grow} becomes competitive mainly when new units have enough time to integrate. Together, these results suggest that \textsc{Grow} should be evaluated not only as an architecture-search operator, but as a time-sensitive optimization process whose success depends on insertion stability.

</details>


### [408] [Visual-Redundancy-Controlled Parallel Decoding for Diffusion-Based Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.25820)
*Yulin Yuan,Hongshuo Zhao,Xiangming Meng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出视觉冗余控制解码（VRCD），一种无需训练的推理时解码方法，通过token-图像注意力优先选择视觉互补的位置，解决扩散型多模态大语言模型并行解码中的视觉冗余问题。实验表明VRCD能降低视觉冗余和剩余位置熵，在M^3CoT和MMBench上相对准确率提升达18.8%和6.9%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于置信度的并行解码策略独立排序掩码位置并提交top-K，忽略了并行提交的token可能依赖重叠的视觉基础，导致视觉冗余，使后续解码可用的互补视觉信息减少。

Method: 1) 提出视觉冗余指数（VRI）量化并行提交token间的视觉基础重叠程度；2) 提出VRCD，利用token-图像注意力优先选择视觉互补的位置进行解码，无需额外训练。

Result: VRCD降低了视觉冗余和剩余位置熵，计算开销适中。在更长解码实验中，相比基于置信度的解码，在M^3CoT上相对准确率提升18.8%，在MMBench上提升6.9%。

Conclusion: VRCD作为一种无需训练的解码方法，有效控制了多模态大语言模型并行解码中的视觉冗余问题，提升了多模态任务的解码质量和准确性。

Abstract: Diffusion-based multimodal large language models (dMLLMs) decode by iteratively predicting tokens at multiple masked positions in parallel. This turns each decoding step into a position-selection problem: the model must choose not only which predictions are reliable in isolation, but also which positions should be committed together as context for later decoding steps. Existing confidence-based decoding ranks masked positions independently and commits the top-K positions, largely ignoring whether the committed tokens provide complementary visual grounding. We identify a step-level limitation of this strategy in multimodal settings: high-confidence tokens selected in the same step can rely on overlapping visual grounding, introducing visual redundancy among the committed tokens and leaving less complementary visual grounding available for later decoding. To quantify this effect, we introduce the Visual Redundancy Index (VRI), which measures visual grounding overlap among tokens committed in parallel. To control this redundancy during decoding, we propose Visual-Redundancy-Controlled Decoding (VRCD), a training-free inference-time decoding method that uses token-to-image attention to prioritize visually complementary positions. Across diverse multimodal benchmarks, VRCD reduces visual redundancy and remaining-position entropy with modest runtime overhead. In longer decoding experiments, it also achieves relative accuracy gains of up to 18.8% on M^3CoT and 6.9% on MMBench over confidence-based decoding. Code is available at https://github.com/infiniteYuanyl/VRCD.

</details>


### [409] [When Does Deep RL Beat Calibrated Baselines? A Benchmark Study on Adaptive Resource Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.26418)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Kai Zhao,Shahryar Sarkani,John Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过RLScale-Bench基准测试发现，经过良好校准的基于规则的自动缩放器在成本控制上优于六种主流深度强化学习算法。研究表明DRL在资源控制中的瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励工程和评估协议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑深度强化学习在自适应资源控制场景中的实际有效性，探究当基线经过恰当校准时，DRL是否真的能带来性能提升。

Method: 构建RLScale-Bench可重复基准测试平台，在Kubernetes水平Pod自动缩放场景下，对PPO、DQN、A2C、SAC、TD3、DDPG六种DRL算法与经过校准的基于规则的基线进行对比，涵盖6种工作负载模式和5个随机种子（共240次运行）。

Result: (i) 校准后的控制器在所有六种工作负载上实现最低成本，但在突发和闪崩流量上落后于最佳RL智能体；(ii) 离散动作算法在约束违反上比连续动作算法优1-2个数量级；(iii) 没有单一算法在所有工作负载上占优，排名变动多达4位。

Conclusion: RL在资源控制中的瓶颈不在于算法选择，而在于基线校准、奖励工程和更真实的评估协议。

Abstract: A properly calibrated rule-based autoscaler can beat every one of six mainstream deep reinforcement learning (DRL) algorithms on cost across every workload we test - so when, if ever, does DRL actually help? We study this in RLScale-Bench, a reproducible benchmark and evaluation protocol for DRL on adaptive resource control, where an agent allocates compute to a dynamic workload under cost and service-level constraints. We evaluate PPO, DQN, A2C, SAC, TD3, and DDPG under matched architectures, training budgets, and reward functions against a calibrated rule-based baseline across six workload patterns and five seeds (240 runs), instantiate the benchmark on Kubernetes Horizontal Pod Autoscaling, and probe distribution-shift generalization. Three findings challenge common assumptions: (i) the calibrated controller achieves the lowest cost on all six workloads, though it trails the best RL agents on bursty and flash traffic; (ii) discrete-action algorithms outperform continuous-action ones by one to two orders of magnitude in constraint violations due to action-space mismatch; and (iii) no single algorithm dominates across workloads, with rankings shifting by up to four positions. The bottleneck in RL-based resource control is not algorithm selection but baseline calibration, reward engineering, and realistic evaluation protocols.

</details>


### [410] [Apertus LLM Family Expansion via Distillation and Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.29128)
*Andrei Panferov,Davit Melikidze,Martin Jaggi,Dan Alistarh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文验证了蒸馏和量化是扩展现有模型家族至新规模和新硬件格式的经济有效方法。基于开源Apertus 8B模型，通过蒸馏和量化生产了参数最高达4B的Apertus-v1.1模型家族（在1.7T许可令牌上训练），展示了其在覆盖广泛硬件和系统需求方面的成本效益和强大准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs被广泛应用于聊天助手、数据标注等多种场景，需要满足不同的预算和硬件约束。当前趋势是发布包含不同规模模型的批次以覆盖尽可能广泛的约束范围，但如何高效低成本地扩展模型家族至新规模和新硬件格式是一个关键问题。

Method: 基于开源Apertus 8B LLM，采用蒸馏（distillation）和量化（quantization）技术，在1.7T许可令牌上训练生成参数规模最高达4B的Apertus-v1.1模型家族。

Result: 成功生产了Apertus-v1.1模型家族（最高4B参数），验证了蒸馏和量化在扩展模型家族方面的成本效益，并在覆盖广泛硬件和系统需求方面展现了强准确性表现。

Conclusion: 蒸馏和量化是扩展现有模型家族至新规模和新硬件格式的经济有效途径，能够高效覆盖不同硬件和系统约束需求。

Abstract: The wide adoption of LLMs has led to their use in great variety of applications and scenarios, such as chatbot assistants and data annotation, creating the need for the models to satisfy certain budget and hardware constraints. This has led to the trend of LLMs being released in batches consisting of similar models of various sizes for the family of models to adhere to as wide of a range of constraints as possible. In this paper, we validate distillation and quantization as a cost-effective way to expand model families to new sizes and hardware formats. Based on the open-recipe Apertus 8B LLM, we produce Apertus-v1.1 - a distilled family of models with up to 4B parameters trained on 1.7T permissive license tokens. We demonstrate cost-efficiency and strong accuracy performance of our approach for covering large ranges of hardware and systems requirements.

</details>


### [411] [Geometric Erasure by Contrastive Velocity Matching in Rectified Flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.00140)
*Jonas Henry Grebe,Tobias Braun,Anna Rohrbach,Marcus Rohrbach*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出GEM框架，用于Rectified Flow模型中概念擦除，通过结合生成流网络的轨迹反学习与教师引导擦除，实现无需目标概念的高效擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态生成模型的快速采用增加了有害内容合成、深度伪造和版权侵权的风险。现有概念擦除方法主要针对U-Net扩散模型，但该领域正逐渐向Rectified Flow Transformer过渡，擦除研究未能跟上这一转变。

Method: 建立基于生成流网络的轨迹反学习与经典教师引导擦除之间的桥梁，将轨迹信号转化为教师引导的流匹配设置。教师提供互补的吸引和排斥信号，合并为单一几何引导目标，实现目标概念的定向抑制同时保持良性生成能力。

Result: 提出一个简单但高效的擦除框架，统一了轨迹反学习和教师引导擦除的优势，在抑制不需要概念的同时保持良性生成质量。

Conclusion: GEM框架成功将概念擦除扩展到Rectified Flow模型，通过几何引导目标实现了高效、精准的概念抑制，为生成模型安全防护提供了新方案。

Abstract: While the rapid adoption of multimodal generative models offers immense potential, it has also increased the risks of harmful content synthesis, deepfakes, and copyright infringements. To address these challenges, concept erasure has emerged as a prospective safeguard. However, as the field gradually transitions from U-Net-based diffusion models to Rectified Flow Transformers, erasure research has struggled to keep pace. In this work, we introduce GEM, a simple but highly effective erasure framework for Rectified Flow models. As part of our contribution, we establish a principled bridge between trajectory-based unlearning grounded in Generative Flow Networks and classic teacher-guided erasure: we translate trajectory-based signals into a teacher-guided flow-matching setup that unifies the strengths of both paradigms. Concretely, a teacher provides complementary attraction and repulsion signals that we combine into a single geometric guidance objective, yielding targeted suppression of unwanted concepts while preserving benign generation.

</details>


### [412] [Anomalies in Multivariate Time Series Benchmarks Are Mostly Univariate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.02670)
*Marc Pinet,Julien Cumin,Samuel Berlemont,Dominique Vaufreydaz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过诊断框架评估了多变量时间序列异常检测中跨通道建模的有效性。在八个公开基准上，发现没有跨通道异常能脱离单变量偏差而独立存在，且六个基准中至少半数异常片段在89%-100%的时间步上存在单变量偏差。合成数据验证框架能正确识别跨通道异常，但真实基准上跨通道建模并无增益，结论是当前基准不适合验证跨通道建模能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前MTSAD模型普遍假设异常可能跨多个通道分布并据此进行跨通道建模，但该假设在公共基准数据集上是否成立缺乏系统性验证。

Method: 提出逐片段诊断框架，对每个标注异常判断：1) 是否存在至少一个通道单独偏离其正常历史；2) 跨通道相关结构是否发生变化；3) 两者兼有。并引入补充指标统计单变量偏离的时间步比例。此外构造相位偏移正弦通道合成数据验证框架有效性。

Result: 在八个基准上，无跨通道异常不伴随单变量偏差；六个基准中至少半数异常片段在89%-100%时间步上存在单变量偏差。合成数据上框架能正确识别跨通道异常，真实基准上CD与CI模型性能无显著差异。

Conclusion: 当前MTSAD基准数据集不适合验证跨通道建模能力，需开发结构更多样的评估集。

Abstract: Many recent multivariate time series anomaly detection (MTSAD) models incorporate cross-channel modeling, under the implicit assumption that the structure of anomalies may be spread across multiple channels. We evaluate this assumption on eight widely used public benchmarks by introducing a per-segment diagnostic framework that flags, for each labeled anomaly, whether at least one channel deviates individually from its normal history, whether the cross-channel correlation structure changes, or both. The framework shows that no cross-channel rupture occurs without an accompanying univariate deviation across a range of reasonable thresholds. A complementary metric also reveals that on six of the eight benchmarks, at least half of the labeled anomaly segments deviate univariately on 89% to 100% of their timesteps, reaching 100% on three of these datasets. To verify that our framework captures cross-channel structure when present, we construct synthetic data of phase-shifted sinusoidal channels with shared noise. Each anomalous segment is altered through one of two channel-wise corruptions that preserve the per-channel marginal distribution while breaking cross-channel structure, and our framework correctly characterizes these segments as cross-channel-only. On these data, channel-dependent (CD) models successfully exploit the cross-channel signal whereas channel-independent (CI) ones fail. The CI/CD comparison of a recent SOTA detector on real benchmarks further confirms that CD modeling brings no measurable gain. We conclude that current MTSAD benchmarks are unsuitable for validating cross-channel modeling capabilities, and we call for the development of more structurally diverse evaluation sets. The code for this study is publicly available.

</details>


### [413] [Libra: Efficient Resource Management for Agentic RL Post-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.03077)
*Kaiwen Chen,Xin Tan,Jingzong Li,Hong Xu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Libra是一个面向大语言模型强化学习后训练的资源管理系统，通过全局资源规划器和因果驱动的多级反馈队列调度器，解决rollout与训练阶段之间的资源管理不平衡问题，在48块A800 GPU上实现最高3.0倍的吞吐量提升和2.5倍的奖励收敛加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于强化学习的LLM后训练中，rollout阶段与训练阶段存在显著的计算模式、内存需求和序列长度敏感度不对称性，且序列长度分布随策略演化持续漂移，导致静态资源分配方案逐渐次优。同时，长尾分布使得少量轨迹主导rollout完成时间。现有系统缺乏对这两类挑战的有效应对。

Method: Libra包含两个核心机制：(1) 全局资源规划器，联合优化rollout集群和训练集群间的GPU分配，通过弹性混合池实现轻量级、非阻塞的阶段性工作者重分配；(2) 因果驱动的多级反馈队列(C-MLFQ)调度器，基于工具返回结果的因果信号将请求路由到异构rollout桶中，而非依赖脆弱的长尾预测。

Result: 在48块A800 GPU上的实验评估中，相比基线方案，Libra实现了最高3.0倍的吞吐量提升和2.5倍的奖励收敛加速。

Conclusion: Libra通过全局资源规划与因果驱动的调度机制，有效解决了LLM强化学习后训练中的资源管理不平衡问题，显著提升了训练效率和性能。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has emerged as a standard post-training paradigm for shaping large language models (LLMs) into capable agents. In agentic RL, the rollout stage generates trajectories while invoking tools, producing long-tailed and non-stationary workloads that expose two fundamental challenges in resource management. First, due to the long-tail distribution, a small fraction of trajectories dominates rollout makespan. Second, rollout and training are subject to cross-stage imbalance, as they exhibit strong asymmetry in compute patterns, memory demands, and sensitivity to sequence length. Compounding this asymmetry, the sequence length distribution drifts continuously as the policy evolves, rendering any static resource split progressively suboptimal.
  We present Libra, a resource management system to address both challenges via two core mechanisms. The first is a global resource planner that jointly optimizes GPU allocation across rollout and training clusters. It leverages an elastic hybrid pool to enable lightweight, non-blocking worker reallocation between stages. The second is a causality-driven multi-level feedback queue (C-MLFQ) scheduler, which routes requests to heterogeneous rollout buckets based on causal signals derived from tool-return outcomes, rather than relying on fragile length predictions. Evaluated on 48 A800 GPUs, Libra achieves up to 3.0x higher throughput and converges up to 2.5x faster in reward compared to the baselines.

</details>


### [414] [EvalStop: Using World Feedback to Detect and Correct Reward Overoptimization in Multi-Tenant RLHF Platforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.04145)
*Guilin Zhang,Chuanyi Sun,Shahryar Sarkani,John M. Fossaceca*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出EvalStop调度原语，通过监控评估分数连续下降来终止RLHF作业中的奖励过度优化（reward hacking），释放GPU并保留最佳检查点。在RLHF密集型工作负载下，精度98%/召回99%/误报率1.5%，同时JCT提升9%，计算浪费减少22%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的云LLM微调平台调度器无法处理RLHF工作负载中的奖励过度优化问题：非先知调度器完全不考虑质量信号，SLAQ式调度器使用训练损失（可被hacking简单降低），而传统的每作业早停需要人工监控且不释放共享GPU。

Method: 将调度器级别的早停建模为检测问题，提出EvalStop原语：监控评估分数连续k次下降时终止作业，释放GPU，保留最佳检查点，并可委托给任意基础调度器。在离散事件模拟器中混合奖励hacking和健康RLHF运行进行评测。

Result: RLHF密集型负载（80% RLHF, 64 GPU）下：精度98%/召回99%/误报率1.5%，JCT提升9%，计算浪费减少22%（vs SRTF-Est, p<0.05）。所有基础调度器上均有9-25% JCT提升。评估噪声标准差≤0.05时精度≥91%，hacking比例20-80%时精度≥89%。

Conclusion: EvalStop作为可组合的调度原语，能有效检测奖励过度优化，显著提升调度效率，且在不同基础调度器、噪声水平和hacking比例下均保持稳定的检测质量。

Abstract: Cloud LLM fine-tuning platforms increasingly serve RLHF workloads, where a learned reward model is optimized as a proxy for human quality. As Gao et al. (2023) showed, this proxy diverges from world feedback (downstream eval metrics) under sustained optimization pressure, a phenomenon known as reward overoptimization. Existing platform schedulers ignore this divergence: non-clairvoyant schedulers optimize JCT without any quality signal, SLAQ-style quality-aware schedulers use training loss (a weaker proxy that drops monotonically through hacking), and classical per-job early stopping requires human monitoring and does not free shared GPUs. We propose EvalStop, a composable scheduling primitive that terminates jobs on k consecutive eval-score declines, releases GPUs, preserves the best checkpoint, and delegates to any base scheduler. We frame scheduler-level early stopping as a detection problem and evaluate it in a discrete-event simulator whose RLHF workload mixes reward-hacking and structurally healthy runs, with ground-truth labels hidden from schedulers. On RLHF-heavy workloads (80% RLHF, 64 GPUs), EvalStop achieves precision 98% / recall 99% / FPR 1.5% while improving JCT by 9% and cutting wasted compute by 22% over SRTF-Est (p<0.05). Trivial fixed-progress and loss-plateau competitors either incur 65% FPR on healthy RLHF or miss over half of true hacking cases. Gains compose across every base scheduler tested (9-25% JCT) and detection quality stays stable under eval noise (precision at least 91% at noise std <= 0.05) and hacking base rate (precision at least 89% across 20-80% hacking fractions).

</details>


### [415] [On the Geometry of On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07082)
*Zhennan Shen,Yanshu Li,Qingyu Yin,Chak Tou Leong,Zhilin Wang,Yanxu Chen,Rongduo Han,Sunbowen Lee,Yi R. Fung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统刻画了在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在参数空间中的更新轨迹，发现其与监督微调（SFT）和可验证奖励强化学习（RLVR）存在本质差异：OPD更新更稀疏、更偏离主方向、且表现出子空间锁定现象——累计更新迅速进入一个低维通道，该子空间对OPD足够但会显著降低SFT性能。控制实验表明，令牌稀疏化和离线rollout生成不影响秩动力学，但混合RLVR目标会改变它。结论是OPD并非SFT和RLVR的简单中间态，而是具有独特的参数空间更新几何。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线策略蒸馏（OPD）在提升大语言模型推理能力方面应用日益广泛，但其训练动态机制尚不清晰。作者希望通过参数空间分析，揭示OPD与SFT、RLVR在更新轨迹上的本质差异，从而加深对OPD训练过程的理解。

Method: 作者设计了一套参数空间诊断指标，对比OPD、SFT和RLVR三种训练方法的更新特征；通过分析更新权重的稀疏性、主方向偏离程度、累计更新的子空间锁定现象，以及控制实验（令牌稀疏化、离线rollout、混合RLVR目标）来揭示OPD的独特更新几何。

Result: OPD更新更稀疏、更偏离主方向（松弛的离主方向机制）；与RLVR相比约束更少；表现出子空间锁定——累计更新迅速进入低维通道；该锁定子空间对OPD性能足够但对SFT显著不足；令牌稀疏化和离线rollout不影响秩动力学，但混合RLVR目标会改变它。

Conclusion: OPD并非SFT和RLVR之间的简单中间点，而是在参数空间中具有自身独特的更新几何结构，这为理解和优化OPD训练提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) is increasingly used to improve large language model reasoning, but its training dynamics remain poorly understood. We characterize the trajectory of OPD updates in parameter space and compare it with supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR). A suite of parameter-space diagnostics consistently places OPD in a relaxed off-principal regime: compared with SFT, its updates affect fewer weights and avoid principal directions more strongly, while compared with RLVR, they remain less tightly constrained. Beyond this static localization, OPD exhibits subspace locking: its cumulative updates rapidly enter a narrow low-dimensional channel. Constraining training to the update subspace formed early in training preserves OPD performance but substantially degrades SFT, indicating that the locked subspace is functionally sufficient for OPD. Control experiments further show that sparsifying the update tokens and shifting rollout generation off-policy preserve the rank dynamics, whereas mixing the OPD objective with RLVR changes them. Overall, these results suggest that OPD is not merely an intermediate point between SFT and RLVR, but induces its own update geometry in parameter space.

</details>


### [416] [DEFINED: A Data-Efficient Computational Framework for Fine-Grained Creativity Assessment in Debate Scenarios](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07226)
*Tongzhou Yu,Mingjia Li,Hong Qian,Wenkai Wang,Zongbao Zhang,Yaoyu Jiang,Xiangfeng Wang,Aimin Zhou,Jiajun Guo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出DEFINED，一个面向辩论场景的细粒度创造力评估框架，通过八维指标体系、层级化评分头、约束数据增强和混合粒度训练策略，在有限专家标注数据下实现准确稳定的创造力评分，优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型时代，人类创造力成为关键能力，但现有评估方法依赖标准化简单任务和昂贵的人工评估，缺乏适用于复杂开放场景（如辩论）的自动化细粒度评估方法。

Method: 构建八维辩论创造力指标体系，使用预训练自回归语言模型搭配层级化评分头；采用约束数据增强策略解决精英偏差问题；提出混合粒度训练策略在有限细粒度专家标注下实现鲁棒学习；并纳入实证研究验证生态效度。

Result: 评分模型在评估协议中实现准确稳定的评分，优于基于提示的大语言模型评估器和现有辩论评分方法。

Conclusion: DEFINED框架证明了在数据有限条件下对复杂开放场景（如辩论）进行细粒度创造力自动评估的可行性，为生态效度更高的创造力评估提供了新范式。

Abstract: Human creativity has emerged as a critical competency in the era of large language models. Assessing creativity in complex, open-ended environments is a grand challenge in data mining, currently hindered by a reliance on standardized simple tasks and the scarcity of fine-grained expert data. As an ecologically valid assessment context, debate reflects multiple dimensions of creativity, encompassing both divergent thinking and convergent thinking. Moreover, debate is a data-rich domain, with a large volume of publicly accessible materials. Current mainstream automated scoring methods are poorly suited to complex settings such as debate, and therefore still rely on costly human evaluation. To this end, this paper proposes DEFINED, a data-efficient computational framework for fine-grained creativity assessment in debate scenarios. DEFINED operationalizes debate creativity through a hierarchical eight-dimensional metric system, implemented via a pre-trained autoregressive language model with a hierarchical scoring head that supports both fine-grained and coarse-grained evaluation. Statements and their associated expert scores were obtained from authentic debate competitions, and a constrained data augmentation strategy was employed to address the elite bias inherent in the original data. DEFINED adopts a mixed-granularity training strategy enabling robust learning from limited fine-grained supervision annotated by trained graduate experts. To rigorously validate ecological validity beyond synthetic benchmarks, we incorporate an empirical study with debate-naive participants, utilizing these authentic data to serve as a qualitative case study for mid-to-low proficiency populations. Across our evaluation protocol, our scoring model achieves accurate and stable scoring, outperforming prompt-based large language model evaluators and existing debate scoring methods.

</details>


### [417] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对vLLM推理引擎的冷启动延迟进行了系统性性能表征，将启动过程分解为六个基本步骤，发现其主要为CPU密集型，建立了预测启动延迟的轻量级分析模型，并开源了相关工具和数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得日益重要。vLLM已成为主流推理引擎，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，此前缺乏对其启动延迟的系统性研究。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基本步骤，分析各步骤相对于模型级和系统级参数的一致且可解释的扩展趋势，实现延迟源的细粒度归因。在此基础上构建轻量级分析模型。

Result: 证明了vLLM启动过程主要为CPU密集型；每个步骤展现出与模型和系统参数相关的一致扩展趋势；所建分析模型能够准确预测给定硬件配置下的启动延迟。

Conclusion: 该研究为大规模推理环境中的资源规划提供了可操作的指导；所有基准数据集、分析工具和预测脚本均已开源。

Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

</details>


### [418] [ResearchClawBench: A Benchmark for End-to-End Autonomous Scientific Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07591)
*Wanghan Xu,Shuo Li,Tianlin Ye,Qinglong Cao,Yixin Chen,Hengjian Gao,Yiheng Wang,Qi Li,Kun Li,Sheng Xu,Shengdu Chai,Fangchen Yu,Xiangyu Zhao,Zhangrui Zhao,Weijie Ma,Zijie Guo,Haoyu Zhou,Haoxiang Yin,Lixue Cheng,Chaofan Hu,Haoxuan Li,Lu Mi,Xuxuan Xie,Yifan Zhou,Ruizhe Chen,Zhiwang Zhou,Xingjian Guo,Yuhao Zhou,Xuming He,Shengyuan Xu,Xinyu Gu,Jiamin Wu,Mianxin Liu,Chunfeng Song,Fenghua Ling,Dongzhan Zhou,Shixiang Tang,Yuqiang Li,Mao Su,Peng Ye,Siqi Sun,Bin Wang,Xue Yang,Zhenfei Yin,Tianfan Fu,Guangtao Zhai,Wanli Ouyang,Bo Zhang,Lei Bai,Wenlong Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ResearchClawBench 是一个评估自主科学研究的基准，包含来自10个科学领域的40个任务。每个任务基于已发表论文，提供原始数据和相关文献，隐藏目标论文进行评估。通过专家策划的多模态评分准则，评估自动化研究智能体（最强为Claude Code，平均21.5分）和原生LLM（最强为Claude-Opus-4.7，平均20.7分），结果显示当前系统远未实现可靠的论文重发现能力，错误集中在实验协议不匹配、证据不匹配和缺乏科学核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI编码智能体越来越多地用于科学工作，但其端到端的自主研究能力仍然难以验证，缺乏可复现的评估基准来衡量自主科学研究的进展。

Method: 构建了一个包含40个任务（覆盖10个科学领域）的基准，每个任务基于真实已发表论文，提供相关文献和原始数据，隐藏目标论文。使用专家策划的多模态评分准则将目标科学成果分解为加权标准，评估目标论文级别的"重新发现"能力。在统一协议下评估7个自主研究智能体和17个原生LLM（通过ResearchHarness）。

Result: 最强自主智能体Claude Code平均得分为21.5，最强ResearchHarness LLM（Claude-Opus-4.7）平均得分为20.7，LLM前沿均值仅为26.5。错误主要源于实验协议不匹配、证据不匹配和缺乏科学核心。

Conclusion: 当前自主研究系统远未实现可靠的论文重发现，ResearchClawBench为衡量自主科学研究进展提供了可复现的评估前沿。

Abstract: AI coding agents are increasingly used for scientific work, but their end-to-end autonomous research capability remains difficult to verify. We present ResearchClawBench, a benchmark for evaluating autonomous scientific research across 40 tasks from 10 scientific domains. Each task is grounded in a real published paper, provides related literature and raw data, and hides the target paper during evaluation. Expert-curated multimodal rubrics decompose the target scientific artifacts into weighted criteria, enabling evaluation of target-paper-level re-discovery while leaving room for new discovery. We evaluate seven autonomous research (auto-research) agents under a unified protocol and seventeen native LLMs through the lightweight ResearchHarness. Current systems remain far from reliable re-discovery: the strongest autonomous agent, Claude Code, averages 21.5, and the strongest ResearchHarness LLM, Claude-Opus-4.7, averages 20.7, with an LLM frontier mean of only 26.5. Error analysis shows that failures concentrate in experimental protocol mismatch, evidence mismatch, and missing scientific core. ResearchClawBench provides a reproducible evaluation frontier for measuring progress toward autonomous scientific research.

</details>


### [419] [GENERIC-FNO: Embedding Energy Conservation and Entropy Production into Fourier Neural Operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08343)
*Jason Sulskis,Sathya Ravi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出GENERIC-FNO，首个将非平衡热力学完整GENERIC（度量-辛）结构嵌入函数空间的神经算子。通过构造精确满足退化条件（能量守恒与熵产生），在任意初始化、维度或分辨率下实现机器精度的结构保持，并在多个PDE基准上取得竞争力结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有结构保持神经算子最多只强制执行单一守恒律或可逆（哈密顿）结构，热力学一致的学习局限于有限维、图或粒子系统。缺乏能够同时嵌入完整非平衡热力学结构（可逆能量守恒+不可逆熵产生+退化条件）的函数空间神经算子。

Method: 将能量和熵泛函学习为神经算子；泊松算子和摩擦算子参数化为对角傅里叶乘子，夹在秩一投影之间，通过构造精确强制执行退化条件，无需惩罚项、更新投影或残差。

Result: 退化恒等式达到机器精度（残差~10^-13）；显式时间步进仅引入O(dt^2)小漂移（每步残差~10^-6）。在1D/2D FNO和DeepONet三种算子骨干、四个PDE（可逆、耗散、混合）上，零样本4倍超分辨率（64到256）保持精确结构保证，恢复物理耗散排序，在多个耗散和混合问题上以相当或更少参数超越强基线。

Conclusion: GENERIC-FNO成功将完整热力学结构嵌入函数空间神经算子，实现了可证明的结构保持与竞争性预测性能，为物理一致性的深度学习算子提供了新范式。同时指出(E,S,L,M)分解不唯一，引入规范无关的耗散诊断来区分可逆与耗散动力学。

Abstract: We introduce GENERIC-FNO, the first neural operator to embed the full GENERIC (metriplectic) structure of nonequilibrium thermodynamics -- reversible, energy-conserving dynamics and irreversible, entropy-producing dynamics coupled through the degeneracy conditions -- directly in function space. Existing structure-preserving neural operators enforce at most a single conservation law or reversible (Hamiltonian) structure, while thermodynamically consistent learning has been confined to finite-dimensional, graph, or particle systems. GENERIC-FNO closes this gap: it learns the energy and entropy functionals as neural operators and parameterizes the Poisson and friction operators as diagonal Fourier multipliers sandwiched between rank-one projections that enforce the degeneracy conditions exactly, by construction, with no penalty term, update projection, or residual. The degeneracy identities hold to machine precision (residuals ~10^-13) for any initialization, dimension, or resolution, so the continuous-time dynamics conserve the learned energy and produce entropy exactly; the explicit time stepping adds only a small O(dt^2) drift (per-step residual ~10^-6). We further note that the (E,S,L,M) decomposition of a given flow is not unique, and introduce a gauge-invariant dissipation diagnostic separating reversible from dissipative dynamics independently of the learned functionals. Across three operator backbones (1D/2D FNOs and DeepONet) and four PDEs spanning reversible, dissipative, and mixed regimes, GENERIC-FNO preserves its exact structural guarantees zero-shot across a 4x super-resolution range (64 to 256), recovers the ground-truth ordering of physical dissipation, and is competitive with strong unconstrained and energy-penalized baselines, outperforming them on several dissipative and mixed problems at comparable or fewer parameters.

</details>


### [420] [From inverse problems to neural operators: prediction, mechanism, and generalization of data-driven models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.08956)
*Conor Rowan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从科学哲学视角，将科学机器学习中多种物理系统建模策略（稀疏识别、神经常微分方程、神经算子等）统一归类为定义输入-输出关系的不同模型类，并基于简约微分方程与机制发现的思想，论证只有某些模型能够实现机制发现与泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习领域涌现了多种看似迥异的物理系统建模方法（如SINDy、Neural ODE、神经算子等），但缺乏一个统一的框架来理解它们之间的关系、差异及各自的适用场景，作者希望从科学哲学的角度对这些方法进行系统分类和理论辨析。

Method: 借鉴科学哲学中关于科学模型和机制的文献，将各种建模策略抽象为定义输入-输出关系的不同模型类；通过分析各方法在模型类假设上的差异，以及物理数据源于简约微分方程这一前提，论证不同模型在机制发现和泛化能力上的本质区别。

Result: 多种建模策略具有共同的结构，仅在所假设的输入-输出关系模型类上存在差异；由于物理数据源于简约微分方程的解，只有那些模型类与潜在微分方程结构一致的建模策略才能实现真正的机制发现和跨输入范围的泛化。

Conclusion: 该分析为理解科学机器学习中各类建模策略提供了统一的哲学框架，阐明了不同方法的内在联系与区别，并为研究人员在不同应用场景下选择适当的建模策略提供了理论指导。

Abstract: Scientists have historically relied on mathematical models based on differential equations to relate system inputs -- forces, fluxes, or heat sources -- to outputs, such as displacement, velocity, concentration, and temperature. These models rely on deep domain knowledge to determine the form of the governing differential equation, which is then calibrated with data by solving an inverse problem. In recent years, the field of Scientific Machine Learning has introduced a variety of alternative modeling strategies for physical systems. A method called Sparse Identification of Nonlinear Dynamics learns the governing equation as a sparse linear combination of terms in a user-defined library. Neural Ordinary Differential Equations construct the governing equation by taking in the state and its derivatives at the input layer of a neural network. Entirely foregoing the modeling framework of differential equations, neural operators directly learn a non-linear mapping between the system inputs and outputs. From inverse problems to neural operators, all of these modeling strategies can be conceptualized as data-driven machinery to predict a system's response over a range of inputs. It is then natural to wonder how exactly these various strategies relate to each other, and whether they can be neatly taxonomized. Drawing from the philosophical literature on scientific models, we argue that many model types have a common structure, differing only in the assumed model class of the input-output relation they define. Connecting to philosophical ideas on mechanism, and arguing that data from physical systems arises from solutions to parsimonious differential equations, we propose that only certain models are capable of mechanism discovery, and thus generalization. Our analysis is intended to unite apparently disparate modeling strategies and provide insight into their appropriate use cases.

</details>


### [421] [Trajectory Geometry of Transformer Representations Across Layers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09287)
*Vishal Pandey,Gopal Singh,Yacine Mahdid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将Transformer前向传播重新建模为高维表示流形上的离散群体轨迹，利用计算神经科学的几何工具，在无需探针的条件下通过五个几何度量（轨迹长度、曲率、语义收敛指数、层间余弦相似度、表示稳定性）分析表示演化。在三个模型家族和五个提示族上发现：语义相关提示在中后层显著收敛、推理任务曲率更大、歧义token轨迹分叉、层间余弦相似度揭示通用三阶段结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Transformer表示在层间如何演化（而非仅仅编码什么）是机械可解释性中的一个开放问题。现有方法多依赖预定义特征的探针分析，缺乏对表示动态几何结构的系统性刻画。

Method: 将Transformer前向传播视为高维表示流形上的离散群体轨迹，借鉴计算神经科学的几何工具。在三个模型（GPT-2、TinyLlama、Qwen2.5）和五个受控提示族上，计算五种直接基于环境空间的几何度量：轨迹长度、曲率、语义收敛指数、层间余弦相似度和表示稳定性。

Result: 四项发现：(1) 语义相关提示在中后层显著收敛（峰值CI 0.41-0.58, p<0.001），类似吸引子动力学；(2) 推理任务曲率（0.71-0.83 rad）大于词汇变化（0.27-0.31 rad）；(3) 歧义token轨迹分叉，最终层表示分离达5.6倍；(4) 层间余弦相似度揭示编码、精化、输出准备的通用三阶段结构。所有效应在打乱层和随机嵌入控制下消失。

Conclusion: 轨迹几何构成一种原则性、无需探针的机械可解释性视角，为理解Transformer表示动态演化提供了新的分析范式。发布了完全开源、模型无关的分析管线。

Abstract: Understanding how transformer representations evolve across layers, not merely what they encode, remains an open problem in mechanistic interpretability. We recast the transformer forward pass as a discrete population trajectory through a high-dimensional representation manifold, drawing on geometric tools from computational neuroscience. Rather than probing for pre-specified features, we characterize trajectory geometry using five metrics computed directly in the ambient space: trajectory length, curvature, a semantic convergence index, layerwise cosine similarity, and representational stability. Across three model families (GPT-2, TinyLlama, Qwen2.5) and five controlled prompt families, we report four findings. First, semantically related prompts converge significantly in middle-to-late layers (peak CI 0.41--0.58, p<0.001, Mann-Whitney U), consistent with attractor-like dynamics. Second, reasoning tasks produce trajectories of greater curvature than lexical variations (0.71--0.83 rad vs. 0.27--0.31 rad), suggesting curvature encodes computational complexity. Third, ambiguous tokens exhibit trajectory bifurcation with up to 5.6x representational separation by the final layer, absent in unambiguous controls. Fourth, layerwise cosine similarity reveals a universal three-phase structure: encoding, elaboration, and output preparation, consistent across all three architectures. All four effects vanish under shuffled-layer and random-embedding controls. We release a fully open-source, model-agnostic pipeline and argue that trajectory geometry constitutes a principled, probe-free lens for mechanistic interpretability.

</details>


### [422] [Intention Driven Identification of In-Possession Match Phases in Association Football through Temporal Graph Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09289)
*Yuesen Li,Daniel Link*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于时序图注意力网络（T-GAN）的框架，利用时空追踪数据识别足球比赛中的控球阶段。该框架定义了三级战术意图和六个阶段，在德甲数据集上取得了良好性能，图建模和序列建模显著提升了分割质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 足球比赛中的控球阶段难以直接观察，且受战术意图驱动而非仅凭空间模式。现有方法缺乏对控球阶段的有效识别，需要一种数据驱动的方法将连续追踪数据转化为战术可解释的阶段表征。

Method: 定义了三级战术意图（侵入对手空间、保持控球、射门）和六个阶段（组织、推进、反击、控球维持、持续威胁、终结）。构建T-GAN模型，结合帧级球员交互图、上下文特征和Transformer时序建模。采用TRACAB系统采集的7场德甲比赛25Hz追踪数据，使用帧级F1和序列感知的IoT-D指标评估。

Result: T-GAN在意图级别达到0.87的宏平均帧级F1，入侵阶段0.76，射门阶段0.79。序列级平均对角IoT-D F1经后处理从0.68提升至0.79（意图），从0.61提升至0.71（阶段）。序列建模是分割质量的主要驱动因素，图关系建模对反击识别特别有益。边路和中场位置组对阶段判别贡献显著。

Conclusion: 该框架能将连续追踪数据转化为战术可解释的控球阶段表征，可用于自动比赛标注、战术分析和打法风格画像等应用。

Abstract: Understanding tactical organisation of association football, hereafter referred to as football, requires identifying distinct match phases. Yet in-possession phases are rarely directly observable and are shaped by evolving tactical intentions, rather than spatial patterns alone. This study proposes a data-driven framework for identifying in-possession match phases from spatiotemporal tracking data. Seven German Bundesliga matches recorded at 25 Hz with TRACAB were analysed. A hierarchical phase model was defined with three tactical intentions (Invade Opponent Space, Keep Possession, Scoring) and six phases (Build Up, Progression, Counter Attack, Maintenance, Sustained Threat, Finishing). A Temporal Graph Attention Network (T-GAN) was developed to combine frame-level player-interaction graphs, contextual features, and Transformer-based temporal modelling. Performance was evaluated using frame-level F1 and a sequence-aware Intersection over Truth-Dominance (IoT-D) metric. T-GAN achieved macro-average frame-level F1 scores of 0.87 at the intention level, 0.76 for invasion-related phases, and 0.79 for scoring phases. At the sequence level, mean diagonal IoT-D F1 increased from 0.68 to 0.79 for intentions and from 0.61 to 0.71 for phases after post-processing, indicating improved temporal coherence. Model comparisons showed that sequence modelling was the main driver of segmentation quality, while graph-based relational modelling was particularly beneficial for Counter Attack recognition. Exploratory player attention analysis further suggested that wide and midfield positional groups contributed strongly to phase discrimination. Overall, the framework translates continuous tracking data into tactically interpretable in-possession phase representations, with potential applications in automated match annotation, tactical analysis, and playing-style profiling.

</details>


### [423] [Learning Dynamics Reveal a Hierarchy of Weight-Induced Layerwise Gram Metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.09744)
*Claudio Nordio*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有固定读出和二次损失的ReLU前馈网络，将梯度下降重新表述为训练集空间上的集体动力学，而非权重空间动力学。单隐层下可消去权重得到残差的封闭方程；深度三层以上则需要权重诱导的Gram算子层级结构实现层间信息传递，共轭场动力学满足后向拉回递归。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将梯度下降动力学从权重空间重新表述为训练集空间上的集体动力学，以揭示深度网络训练的封闭形式演化方程。

Method: 针对ReLU网络（固定读出、二次损失），通过消去权重变量，推导激活动力学和残差动力学的封闭方程，分析不同深度下的核结构及算子层级。

Result: 单隐层下获得残差的封闭方程，核分解为输入几何矩阵与动态共激活矩阵的乘积；深层网络保留逐层核结构，三层以上需引入权重诱导的Gram算子层级；共轭场动力学服从后向拉回递归关系。

Conclusion: 梯度下降动力学可被重新表述为训练集空间上的集体动力学，其封闭形式依赖于深度相关的核结构和算子层级。

Abstract: We study feed-forward ReLU networks with fixed readout and quadratic loss. The aim is to rewrite gradient descent not primarily as a dynamics in weight space, but as a collective dynamics closed in terms of fields defined on the training-set space. For a single hidden layer, the weight variables can be eliminated from the activation dynamics, yielding a closed equation for the residuals governed by a collective kernel that factorizes into an input-geometric matrix and a dynamical co-activation matrix. For deeper networks, the residual dynamics retains a clean layer-wise kernel structure. However, from depth three onward, closure requires a hierarchy of weight-induced Gram operators that mediate information transport across layers. Moreover, the conjugate-field dynamics is governed by operators satisfying a backward pullback recursion, of which the weight-induced Gram operators are the first nontrivial instances.

</details>


### [424] [Density Ridge Selective Prediction for LLM and VLM Hallucination Detection under Calibration Label Scarcity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10198)
*Nina I. Shamsi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于响应流形密度脊的无监督幻觉检测方法，在标注稀缺条件下相比语义熵等方法AUROC提升5-20点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无监督采样检测器（如语义熵）质量 plateau，而有监督探针在标定标签稀缺时性能急剧下降，亟需一种既无需标签又能保持鲁棒性的幻觉检测方法。

Method: 将LLM的响应流形重建为基于隐藏状态生成轨迹六维运动特征图的核密度估计的密度脊；测试生成通过其投影特征点到最近脊顶点的负欧氏距离评分，形成低维几何骨架。

Result: 在6个QA基准、8种文本/视觉LLM上，以n_cal=200、N=5的标签稀缺设置下，基于脊的评分在AUROC上比语义熵、拓扑方法和log概率提升5-20点，且在标定标签稀缺时退化更温和。

Conclusion: 密度脊方法能有效捕捉响应流形的几何结构，在无监督和标签稀缺场景下显著优于现有方法，为幻觉检测提供了一种鲁棒的几何框架。

Abstract: Hallucination detection in large language and vision-language models is increasingly framed as selective prediction, where a detector assigns a confidence score and abstains when confidence is low. Unsupervised sampling detectors (Semantic Entropy) avoid labels but plateau in quality, while supervised probes attain stronger in-distribution scores yet degrade sharply when calibration labels are scarce. We recover the response manifold of an LLM as the density ridge of a kernel density estimate built on a six-dimensional kinematic feature map of hidden state generation trajectories. A test generation is scored by the negated Euclidean distance from its projected feature point to the nearest ridge vertex, yielding a low-dimensional geometric skeleton of the stochastic output distribution. We evaluate against Semantic Entropy, topological methods, and log-probability on six QA benchmarks (HaluEval-QA, TriviaQA, GSM8K, POPE, ScienceQA, A-OKVQA) using eight text and vision LLMs in a deliberately label-scarce protocol ($n_{\text{cal}}{=}200$ queries, $N{=}5$ generations). Our ridge-based score beats on AUROC with 5-20 points gain, while demonstrating tempered degradation under calibration-label scarcity.

</details>


### [425] [Pre-AF 13: An Interpretable Atrial Fibrillation Risk Score Mined from Discharge Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10725)
*Olga Shakhmatova,Dmitrii Kriukov,Daniil Larionov,Nikita Khromov,Iaroslav Bespalov,Alexander Zolotarev,Kirill Grishchenkov,Ekaterina Ivanova,Miron Kuznetsov,Ilya Sochenkov,Elizaveta Panchenko,Artem Shelmanov,Dmitry V. Dylov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究基于俄罗斯单中心电子健康记录，利用NLP从出院报告中提取73个特征，通过LightAutoML构建可解释的机器学习模型（Pre-AF 13和Pre-AF 9），在心血管疾病患者中预测24个月及整个随访期的房颤风险。全模型ROC AUC达0.735（24个月），优于CHARGE-AF等四个临床风险评分（0.53-0.64），年龄和左心房容积为最重要预测因子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有房颤风险评分（如CHARGE-AF、C2HEST等）依赖在心血管疾病患者中几乎普遍存在的因素（如高龄、高血压），在该高危人群中区分度有限；且多数评分针对长期（5-10年）而非中期预测。因此需要针对心血管疾病患者开发中期（24个月）房颤风险预测模型。

Method: 单中心回顾性研究，纳入2012年1月至2019年5月期间多次住院的≥18岁心血管疾病但无房颤的患者。使用自定义NLP流程（规则解析器+Transformer-based NER）从非结构化出院报告中提取73个结构化特征。采用LightAutoML构建全模型（73特征）、简单模型（精简子集）和线性模型（床旁风险评分）。性能评估使用ROC AUC，并与CHARGE-AF、C2HEST、MHS、HAVOC四个临床评分比较，通过SHAP进行模型解释。

Result: 17,562名患者纳入分析，其中1,438例（8.19%）发生房颤。全模型24个月和整个随访期的ROC AUC分别为0.735和0.696；简单模型表现几乎相同（0.725, 0.696）。所有非线性模型均优于四个临床风险评分（ROC AUC 0.53-0.64）。SHAP分析显示年龄和左心房容积为最主要预测因子。简单模型（Pre-AF 13）使用13个特征；线性评分（Pre-AF 9）可将24个月房颤发生率从~7%分层至36%。

Conclusion: 基于常规电子健康记录数据构建的可解释机器学习模型（Pre-AF系列）能够有效识别心血管疾病患者中的高房颤风险人群，其性能优于现有的临床房颤风险评分，为临床中期风险分层提供了实用工具。

Abstract: Background. Atrial fibrillation (AF) is the most prevalent cardiac arrhythmia and a major determinant of prognosis. Established AF risk scores rely on factors (older age, hypertension) nearly ubiquitous among patients with cardiovascular disease (CVD), offering limited stratification in this high-risk group. Most target long-term (5-10 year) rather than medium-term prediction. We developed interpretable ML models predicting AF risk over a 24-month and entire follow-up horizon in CVD patients using routinely collected hospital data.
  Methods. Single-center retrospective study of electronic health records from the National Research Cardiology Center (Russia) for patients aged >=18 with CVD but without pre-existing AF, hospitalized more than once between January 2012 and May 2019. A custom NLP pipeline transformed unstructured discharge reports into 73 structured features, combining a rule-based parser with transformer-based NER. Using LightAutoML we built a full model (73 features), a simple model (reduced subset), and a linear model for a bedside risk score. Performance was assessed by ROC AUC, compared with CHARGE-AF, C2HEST, MHS, and HAVOC, and interpreted via SHAP.
  Results. Of 80,576 records from 45,000 patients, 17,562 met inclusion criteria; 1,438 (8.19%) developed AF. The full model reached ROC AUC 0.735 (24-month) and 0.696 (entire follow-up); the simple model was nearly identical (0.725, 0.696). All non-linear models outperformed the four clinical risk scores (ROC AUC 0.53-0.64). The simple model uses 13 features and is named Pre-AF 13. SHAP identified age and left atrial volume as dominant predictors. A linear risk score (Pre-AF 9) stratified observed 24-month AF incidence from ~7% to 36%.
  Conclusion. Interpretable ML models built from routinely collected EHR data identify high-AF-risk CVD patients, outperforming established clinical risk scores.

</details>


### [426] [K-Forcing: Joint Next-K-Token Decoding via Push-Forward Language Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10820)
*Zhiwei Tang,Yuanyu He,Yizheng Han,Wangbo Zhao,Jiasheng Tang,Fan Wang,Bohan Zhuang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: K-Forcing 是一种前馈式语言建模范式，通过将自回归模型蒸馏为条件前馈映射，实现单次前向传播同时解码多个未来token（k=4时获得约2.4-3.5倍加速），在保持与现有AR推理基础设施兼容的前提下，有效缓解了高负载批处理场景下的推理瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归语言模型逐token解码导致推理过程受内存带宽限制、效率低下，现有加速方法（如推测解码、扩散语言模型）在特定条件下有效，但未能直接解决工业级部署最关键的**高负载批处理服务**场景的加速问题。

Method: 1. 将现有AR模型蒸馏为条件前馈映射（conditional push-forward mapping），将独立的均匀噪声变量转化为多个未来token的联合样本；2. 采用**渐进式自强迫蒸馏**（progressive self-forcing distillation）训练策略，逐步扩展预测窗口，使学生模型紧密匹配AR教师模型的序列分布；3. 保持固定长度输出、复用AR教师骨干网络、兼容标准AR服务基础设施。

Result: 在LM1B和OpenWebText数据集上，使用标准因果Transformer骨干网络，当k=4（每次前向传播生成4个token）时，不同批次大小下获得约**2.4-3.5倍加速**，质量损失相对较小。

Conclusion: K-Forcing 为在真实世界高负载部署场景下加速自回归生成提供了一条有前景的路径，直接回应了推理计算在现代LLM生命周期中占比日益增大的现实挑战。

Abstract: Autoregressive (AR) language modeling is the dominant paradigm for text generation, yet its sequential token-by-token decoding makes inference memory-bound and inefficient. Existing acceleration approaches, such as speculative decoding and diffusion language models, can yield speedups under certain conditions but do not directly address high-load batch serving--the scenario most critical for industrial-scale deployment. We introduce K-Forcing, a push-forward language modeling paradigm for joint next-k-token decoding. K-Forcing distills an existing AR model into a conditional push-forward mapping--one that transforms independent uniform noise variables into a joint sample of multiple future tokens in a single forward pass. This design preserves fixed-length outputs, reuses the AR teacher backbone, and remains compatible with standard AR serving infrastructure. We train this mapping via progressive self-forcing distillation, which gradually expands the prediction window while enabling the student to closely match the sequence distribution of the AR teacher. We evaluate K-Forcing on LM1B and OpenWebText using a standard causal Transformer backbone. When aggressively configured to generate k = 4 tokens per forward pass, K-Forcing delivers approximately 2.4-3.5x speedup across different batch sizes, while incurring modest quality degradation relative to its AR teacher. As inference increasingly dominates the lifetime compute cost of modern LLMs, K-Forcing offers a promising route toward accelerating AR generation under real-world high-load deployment.

</details>


### [427] [Beyond Uniform Token-Level Trust Region in LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.10968)
*Renjie Mao,Xiangxin Zhou,Lvfang Tao,Yixin Ding,Yu Shi,Yongguang Lin,Yuheng Wu,Honglin Zhu,Qian Qiu,Wenxi Zhu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CPPO提出了一种基于累积前缀散度的策略优化方法，通过位置加权阈值和累积前缀预算两个机制，解决了传统PPO在自回归生成中因位置无关的均匀阈值导致的早期偏差累积和后期探索受限问题，显著提升了推理准确性和训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PPO风格的信赖域机制在RLVR中采用位置无关的均匀阈值，忽略了自回归生成的两个关键问题：一是早期偏差会产生累积序列级漂移，导致静态阈值对早期发散约束不足而对后期探索过度约束；二是逐token独立评估散度忽略了前缀累积漂移，使得无论历史偏差程度如何都给予相同的散度容限。

Method: 提出CPPO算法，包含两个核心机制：(1) 位置加权阈值——对早期位置施加更严格限制，因其影响持续更久，同时放松对后期token的约束；(2) 累积前缀预算——追踪历史偏差动态限制后续token的偏离程度，防止前缀上的误差叠加。

Result: 实验表明CPPO在多种模型规模下均能提升训练稳定性和推理准确性。

Conclusion: CPPO通过引入位置感知的累积前缀散度约束，有效对齐了RLVR中策略优化与自回归生成的时序特性，为改进LLM推理能力提供了更稳定的训练方法。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has become standard for improving LLM reasoning. However, existing PPO-style trust-region mechanisms remain position-agnostic by enforcing uniform thresholds across all tokens independently. This pointwise treatment conflicts with autoregressive generation in two critical ways. First, uniform thresholds ignore autoregressive asymmetry. Early-stage deviations produce compounding sequence-level drift, causing static thresholds to under-regulate early divergence and excessively constrain late-stage exploration. Second, evaluating token-level divergence in isolation overlooks cumulative prefix drift, granting the same divergence allowance regardless of how far the conditioning history has already deviated from the rollout policy. To address this limitation, we propose CPPO (Cumulative Prefix-divergence Policy Optimization), a token-level masking rule that aligns updates with a finite-horizon policy-improvement bound via two coupled mechanisms. First, a position-weighted threshold imposes stricter limits at early positions whose effects persist longer, relaxing constraints for late-stage tokens. Second, a cumulative prefix budget tracks historical deviations, dynamically restricting further token-level deviation to prevent compounding errors along the prefix. Empirically, CPPO enhances training stability and significantly improves reasoning accuracy across various model scales.

</details>


### [428] [Data-Driven Dynamic Assortment in Online Platforms: Learning about Two Sides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11118)
*Rahul Roy,Nur Sunar,Jayashankar M. Swaminathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究双边服务平台上的动态分类问题，其中平台不了解顾客和卖家的选择模型参数，提出了一个数据驱动算法，实现了随时间呈多对数增长的遗憾值，并证明了其最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双边服务平台（如共享经济平台）中，平台需要同时匹配顾客和卖家，但双方的选择偏好参数均为未知，现有研究尚未同时处理双方参数未知的动态分类问题。

Method: 构建离散时间动态分类模型，顾客和卖家分别基于MNL模型选择。提出数据驱动算法同时学习双方选择参数并优化平台目标，采用遗憾值（相对于知道全部参数和顾客到达顺序的先知基准）评估性能。

Result: 算法在最坏情况下的遗憾值随时间呈多对数增长，并推导出匹配的下界，证明了算法的率最优性。

Conclusion: 首次解决了双边选择参数均未知的动态分类问题，提出的算法在理论上达到了最优的遗憾增长速率。

Abstract: We study a dynamic assortment problem on a two-sided service platform with incomplete information and heterogeneous customers in a discrete-time setting. In each period, a customer arrives seeking service, and the platform chooses an assortment of sellers to display. The customer then proposes a transaction to at most one seller in the assortment according to a multinomial logit choice model. After a fixed number of periods, sellers review the proposals they have received and each chooses at most one customer according to another multinomial logit choice model, after which the cycle repeats. A key challenge is that the platform does not know the choice-model parameters of either customers or sellers in advance. To our knowledge, this is the first study of a dynamic assortment problem in which both sides' choice parameters are unknown. We develop a data-driven algorithm that learns these parameters while optimizing the platform's objective over time. We evaluate performance using regret, which measures revenue loss relative to a clairvoyant benchmark that knows all parameters and customer arrivals in advance. We show that the algorithm's worst-case regret grows polylogarithmically over time, and we derive a matching lower bound, establishing its rate optimality.

</details>


<div id='q-bio.NC'></div>

# q-bio.NC [[Back]](#toc)

### [429] [Spatially Masked Regression Reveals Local and Distributed Predictability in Electrophysiological Recordings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11415)
*Maryam Ostadsharif Memar,Nima Dehghani*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出空间掩码回归（SMR）框架，通过逐步排除邻近电极来量化神经记录中信号在局部与分布式信息之间的平衡。应用于颅内脑电图（iEEG）和头皮脑电图（EEG）后发现：两种模态均存在强受试者内重建；排除邻近通道后仍有显著残差可预测性；EEG的跨受试者迁移明显强于iEEG。破坏时序或相位结构的替代数据会显著降低性能，表明SMR依赖于结构化时序与跨通道组织。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经记录通常被解读为局部测量，但单一传感器的信号也可能反映更广泛网络中的结构化活动。这提出了一个基本问题：电极信号在多大程度上反映局部信息与分布式信息？具体而言，电极活动有多少由其邻近区域承载，又有多少嵌入在整个阵列中？

Method: 提出Spatially Masked Regression (SMR)框架：从其余电极重建每个电极的时间序列，同时排除目标电极周围可配置的邻域。通过逐步增大掩码，将空间局部性作为实验控制变量，量化排除邻近通道后仍存留的预测信息。应用于颅内脑电图（iEEG）和头皮脑电图（EEG），使用距离相关性评估原始与重建信号之间的相似度。

Result: 两种模态均表现出强受试者内重建；排除邻近邻居后仍有显著残差可预测性；EEG的跨受试者迁移显著强于iEEG。掩码分析表明邻近电极对重建贡献大但并非全部，单个通道既反映局部冗余也反映更广泛的分布式结构。破坏相位结构或时序排序的替代数据会显著降低性能。

Conclusion: SMR是一个可解释的框架，可用于量化神经记录中局部与分布式信息之间的平衡。结果表明单个通道的信号同时包含局部冗余和更广泛的分布式结构，且SMR依赖于结构化时序和跨通道组织，而非仅依赖边缘统计特征。

Abstract: Neural recordings are often interpreted as local measurements, yet the signal at any one sensor can also reflect structured activity distributed across the broader network. This raises a basic question: to what extent does an electrode's signal reflect local versus distributed information in the underlying system? More specifically, how much of an electrode's activity is carried by its immediate neighborhood, and how much is embedded more broadly across the array? We address this with a Spatially Masked Regression (SMR) framework that reconstructs each electrode's timeseries from the remaining electrodes while excluding a configurable neighborhood around the target. By progressively increasing this mask, spatial locality becomes an experimental control for quantifying how much predictive information survives after nearby channels are withheld. We apply SMR to intracranial EEG with heterogeneous electrode coverage and to scalp EEG with standardized montages over sensorimotor cortex. Using distance correlation between original and reconstructed signals, we find strong within-subject reconstruction in both modalities, substantial residual predictability even when local neighbors are excluded, and markedly stronger cross-subject transfer in EEG than in iEEG. Masking shows that nearby electrodes contribute strongly to reconstruction but do not account for all of it, indicating that individual channels reflect both local redundancy and broader distributed structure. Surrogates that preserve selected marginal or spectral properties while disrupting phase structure or temporal ordering substantially reduce performance, supporting the conclusion that SMR depends on structured temporal and cross-channel organization rather than on marginal statistics alone. These results position SMR as an interpretable framework for quantifying the balance between local and distributed information in recordings.

</details>


### [430] [End-to-End Machine Learning for Depressive State Classification via EEG and fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11555)
*Riki Sakurai,Simon Kojima,Mihoko Otake-Matsuura,Shin'ichiro Kanoh,Tomasz M. Rutkowski*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本文提出利用脑电信号(EEG)和功能性近红外光谱(fNIRS)等生物信号进行抑郁症检测的框架，以克服传统精神科诊断中主观偏差和经验判断的局限性，并在11名健康学生的初步研究中验证了可行性，为临床自动化客观诊断工具奠定基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社会压力增加导致心理健康需求上升，传统精神科诊断依赖临床访谈和患者自我报告，存在主观偏差和经验判断差异的固有限制。需要定量、客观的评估方法，特别是对于个体自身可能未识别的潜在抑郁状态，以及在老龄化人群中抑郁症与痴呆症的高共病性需早期区分。

Method: 利用脑电图(EEG)和功能性近红外光谱(fNIRS)等生物信号进行抑郁检测，开展包含11名健康学生的试点研究，建立基于生物信号的抑郁检测框架。

Result: 在11名健康学生的初步研究中成功建立了基于生物信号的抑郁检测框架，为临床自动化客观诊断工具的后续开发奠定了基础。

Conclusion: 生物信号（EEG和fNIRS）为基础的检测方法有望成为传统主观诊断的客观替代方案，本研究作为迈向临床自动化诊断工具的基础性步骤，对识别潜在抑郁状态和老年人群抑郁-痴呆共病区分具有重要价值。

Abstract: The escalating demand for mental healthcare, driven by rising societal stress, highlights the limitations of traditional psychiatric diagnostics. Conventional methods - relying primarily on clinical interviews and patient self-reports - are inherently vulnerable to subjective bias and the varying empirical judgment of practitioners. To address the need for quantitative evaluation, biological signal-based detection, including electroencephalography (EEG) and functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS), has emerged as a promising objective alternative. Such technology is particularly vital for identifying latent depressive states that may be unrecognized by the subjects themselves. Furthermore, in aging populations, the high comorbidity between depression and dementia necessitates early differentiation to prevent mutual symptom exacerbation and maintain Quality of Life (QoL). This pilot study of eleven healthy students establishes a framework for biological signal-based depression detection, serving as a foundational step toward automated, objective diagnostic tools for clinical use.

</details>


### [431] [Large language models selectively converge with human-shared neural semantic representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.11598)
*Chen Hong,Ximing Shao,Gangyi Feng*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 本研究结合MEG超扫描与维度解析的跨脑编码建模，比较人类与大语言模型（LLMs）在共享神经语义表征中的维度结构。发现共享语义是多维神经结构而非单一信号，LLMs虽能部分捕捉人类共享语义，但在能动性、情感和社会经验等维度上存在显著差距，且更大模型更接近人类但仍有维度依赖性偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人际交流需要建立共享语义以实现彼此理解，但这种共享神经表征的维度结构尚不清楚。LLMs日益逼近人类语言能力，但其是否捕获了人脑间共享的相同语义结构仍待解答。

Method: 采用讲故事-听故事伪超扫描MEG实验，结合维度解析的跨脑编码建模。说话者叙事中的实义词由人类和五个近期LLMs在十个语义维度（感知、运动、空间、时间、社会性、生命性、情绪、注意、因果、驱力）上进行评分，检验这些维度是否能解释说话者-听者神经同步（NS），并控制声学和音韵特征。

Result: 人类和LLM衍生的语义空间均能解释NS，但共享语义是多维神经结构而非单一全局信号；这些模式还能预测听者故事理解的个体差异。然而，总体上相当的预测能力掩盖了表征几何的系统性差异：更大LLM与人类在语义结构和NS上更接近且重叠更大，但这种对齐是不完全的且具有维度依赖性，在能动性、情感和社会经验相关维度上差异最大。

Conclusion: LLMs能够捕捉人类共享神经语义的实质性成分，但其对齐具有选择性。更大或更强的模型能改进近似程度，但社会和情感扎根的维度仅被部分捕捉。

Abstract: Interpersonal communication requires building shared semantics that enable listeners to understand speakers' meanings from their unfolding language, but the dimensional structure of this shared neural representation remains unclear. LLMs increasingly approximate human language capability and neural responses, raising the question of whether they capture the same semantic structure shared between human brains. Here, we combined storytelling-listening pseudo-hyperscanning MEG with dimension-resolved interbrain encoding modeling to compare human- and LLM-derived accounts of shared neural semantic representations. Content words from the speaker's narratives were rated by humans and five recent LLMs along ten semantic dimensions (i.e., perception, motor, space, time, socialness, animacy, emotion, attention, causality, and drive). We tested whether these dimensions explained speaker-listener neural synchronization (NS) beyond acoustic and phonological features. Both human- and LLM-derived semantic spaces explained NS, but these shared semantics are better characterized as a multidimensional neural structure rather than a single global signal. These patterns also predicted individual differences in listeners' story comprehension, linking neural alignment to cognition. However, comparable overall prediction concealed systematic differences in representational geometry. Larger LLMs aligned more closely and showed greater overlap with humans in semantic structure and NS, but this was incomplete and dimension-dependent. The largest divergences emerged for dimensions closely tied to agency, affect, and social experience. These findings show that LLMs capture substantial components of human shared neural semantics, but their alignment is selective. Larger or more capable models improve the approximation, whereas socially and affectively grounded dimensions are captured only partially.

</details>
